# 2027 New Grad Positions by Understudy

[![Last refreshed](https://img.shields.io/github/last-commit/Keugene11/New-Grad-Tech-Jobs?label=last%20refreshed&style=flat-square&color=0a7cff)](https://github.com/Keugene11/New-Grad-Tech-Jobs/commits/main) ![Open roles](https://img.shields.io/badge/open%20roles-557-brightgreen?style=flat-square) ![Companies](https://img.shields.io/badge/companies-314-blue?style=flat-square) [![Applies for you](https://img.shields.io/badge/apply%20automatically-Understudy-black?style=flat-square)](https://understudy.live/?utm_source=ghlist&utm_medium=badge)

**557 open entry-level and new-grad roles for the class of 2027 in software, product, data, AI/ML, quant and hardware, across 314 companies.**

Generated straight from company job boards by [Understudy](https://understudy.live/?utm_source=ghlist&utm_medium=title) — no hand-curation, no agency reposts, no dead links left sitting for weeks. A role appears here within minutes of going up on the employer's own board, and disappears when the board stops listing it.

🙏 **Missing a company? [Open an issue](https://github.com/Keugene11/New-Grad-Tech-Jobs/issues/new/choose) and the board joins the crawl.** See [CONTRIBUTING.md](./CONTRIBUTING.md). 🙏

## Legend

🔥 Big-tech / top-tier company

🎓 Advanced degree required (Master's, PhD, MBA)

🛂 Does NOT offer sponsorship

🇺🇸 Requires U.S. Citizenship

🏠 Remote or remote-friendly

<img src="https://understudy.live/lists/auto-apply.svg" width="78" alt="Auto-apply"> — Understudy fills in and submits that application for you, with your resume. Free to start.

**Age** — days since the employer posted it. `0d` went up today.

> **Sponsorship and citizenship** flags (🛂 🇺🇸) are shown only where a posting is known to state them. No flag
> does not mean sponsorship is offered; the posting itself has the last word.

## FAQ

<details>
<summary><strong>Where do these come from?</strong></summary>

Understudy crawls the public job boards of about 20,000 companies — Greenhouse, Lever, Ashby, Workable,
SmartRecruiters, Recruitee, Workday, Oracle, iCIMS and Eightfold, plus Amazon's and Microsoft's own sites — on a
rolling schedule, with the boards that post most often re-checked every few minutes. These tables are a filtered
view of that crawl: open postings, published in the last 7 days, whose title says the role is for a new graduate, entry level or level I, that SimplifyJobs also lists, or whose description says so ("0–2 years of experience", "this is an early-career role").

Because it reads the employer's own board, every link here is the real application page. There is no
intermediate posting that can go stale.
</details>

<details>
<summary><strong>How is a role classified?</strong></summary>

By title, with the rules in [`src/lib/lists/roles.ts`](https://github.com/Keugene11/understudy) in the
Understudy repo. Categories are matched in priority order, so a "Machine Learning Software Engineer" is filed
under Data/AI rather than Software Engineering. Non-software engineering disciplines (mechanical,
manufacturing, civil, industrial, chemical) are excluded, as are part-time, contract and student-worker
postings.

Classification by title is imperfect by nature. If something is in the wrong section, open an issue.
</details>

<details>
<summary><strong>Can I use this data?</strong></summary>

Yes. [`.github/scripts/listings.json`](./.github/scripts/listings.json) is the machine-readable version, and
it uses the same field names as the SimplifyJobs lists — so tooling written for those repos reads this one
without changes.
</details>

<details>
<summary><strong>Does applying through here cost anything?</strong></summary>

No. Every **Apply** link goes directly to the employer. **Auto-apply** is the optional other way: it opens
Understudy, which fills in and submits that application for you instead of you doing it by hand.
</details>
